class SandboxHandle:
    """Opaque per-task handle returned from ``SandboxRuntime.start``.

    Carries the workspace directory + any runtime-specific bookkeeping
    needed for ``execute`` / ``stop``.
    """

    def __init__(self, workspace: Path, runtime_id: str = "",
                 metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
        self.workspace = workspace
        self.runtime_id = runtime_id
        self.metadata: dict[str, Any] = metadata or {}


class SandboxRuntime(Protocol):
    """Workspace + execution sandbox for a single task.

    Implementations MUST honour ``allowed_paths``: nothing outside that
    set should be physically reachable from inside the sandbox.  The
    default ``LocalSandboxRuntime`` enforces this by copying-only the
    allowed paths into a tmp workspace and refusing the launch when an
    allowed glob escapes the project root.
    """

    def start(self, *, worker_id: str, task_id: str,
              card: TaskCard, project_root: Path) -> SandboxHandle: ...

    def commit(self, handle: SandboxHandle, *,
               commit_message: str) -> str: ...

    def stop(self, handle: SandboxHandle) -> None: ...


class AgentExecutor(Protocol):
    """Runs the agent step inside a prepared sandbox workspace."""

    def run(self, *, handle: SandboxHandle, card: TaskCard,
            worker_id: str) -> AgentResult: ...


class GerritPusher(Protocol):
    """Pushes a sandbox commit out for review."""

    def push(self, *, handle: SandboxHandle, card: TaskCard,
             commit_sha: str, change_id: str,
             worker_id: str) -> GerritPushResult: ...


class HeartbeatStore(Protocol):
    """Backs ``worker:<id>:alive`` + ``workers:active`` set.

    The default implementation prefers Redis when ``OMNISIGHT_REDIS_URL``
    is set; otherwise falls back to an in-memory dict so single-process
    tests / dev runs work without Redis.
    """

    def register(self, worker_id: str, info: dict[str, Any],
                 ttl_s: int) -> None: ...

    def heartbeat(self, worker_id: str, info: dict[str, Any],
                  ttl_s: int) -> bool: ...

    def deregister(self, worker_id: str) -> None: ...

    def list_active(self) -> list[str]: ...

    def get_info(self, worker_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None: ...


# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
#  Default runtime implementations
# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━


class _StubAgentExecutor:
    """Default executor — records the invocation, no real agent call.

    Used by the integration test suite and by ``--dry-run`` mode so
    the worker can be exercised end-to-end without an LLM key.  Real
    deployments inject the production executor at construction time.
    """

    def run(self, *, handle: SandboxHandle, card: TaskCard,
            worker_id: str) -> AgentResult:
        marker = handle.workspace / ".omnisight-stub-agent.json"
        marker.write_text(json.dumps({
            "worker_id": worker_id,
            "ticket": card.jira_ticket,
            "entry_point": card.navigation.entry_point,
        }, indent=2))
        return AgentResult(
            ok=True,
            commit_message=(
                f"stub-agent: {card.jira_ticket}\n\n"
                f"{card.acceptance_criteria}\n"
            ),
            files_touched=[str(marker.relative_to(handle.workspace))],
        )


class LocalSandboxRuntime:
    """Filesystem sandbox.  No Docker required.

    ``start`` materialises a fresh workspace directory + clones a thin
    tree containing **only** the files matched by the CATC card's
    ``impact_scope.allowed`` globs.  Anything else (other source tree,
    secrets, sibling repos) is physically unreachable from the
    workspace because it was never copied in.

    The worker uses ``git`` inside the workspace to commit; ``commit``
    runs ``git add -A && git commit -m ...`` in the workspace dir and
    returns the resulting SHA.  This keeps the local backend trivially
    testable while preserving the spec's "bind-mount only allowed
    paths" guarantee (the implementation IS the bind, just via copy).
    """

    def __init__(self, *, workdir_root: Path | None = None,
                 init_git: bool = True) -> None:
        self._root = Path(workdir_root or
                          os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_WORKER_WORKDIR")
                          or Path.cwd() / ".artifacts" / "worker_sandboxes")
        self._init_git = init_git
        self._root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def start(self, *, worker_id: str, task_id: str,
              card: TaskCard, project_root: Path) -> SandboxHandle:
        ws = self._root / f"{worker_id}-{task_id}"
        if ws.exists():
            _rmtree(ws)
        ws.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        allowed = list(card.navigation.impact_scope.allowed)
        copied: list[str] = []
        for glob in allowed:
            for src in _resolve_glob(project_root, glob):
                rel = src.relative_to(project_root)
                dst = ws / rel
                dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                if src.is_dir():
                    _copytree(src, dst)
                else:
                    _copyfile(src, dst)
                copied.append(str(rel))

        if self._init_git:
            _run_git(ws, ["init", "-q", "-b", "main"])
    db_l = (db or "").lower()
    user_l = (user or "").lower()

    if db_l.endswith(("_dev", "-dev")) or user_l == "omnisight_dev" or (port in _DEV_PORTS):
        return "dev"
    if db_l.endswith(("_staging", "-staging")) or (port in _STAGING_PORTS):
        return "staging"
    if db_l == "omnisight" and user_l == "omnisight":
        return "prod"
    return "unknown"


def _truthy(val: str | None) -> bool:
    return (val or "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")


def enforce_env_db_contract(
    dsn: str | None,
    *,
    source: str,
    env: str | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Fail closed (exit 78) when ``OMNISIGHT_ENV`` disagrees with ``dsn``.

    ``source`` is a short label for the call site (for the error/log message).
    ``env`` overrides ``OMNISIGHT_ENV`` (used by tests). See the design doc for
    the full truth table; summary:

    ===============  ==========  =======
    OMNISIGHT_ENV    DSN class   verdict
    ===============  ==========  =======
    dev              dev          OK
    dev              prod/stg     FAIL  (core protection)
    prod             prod         OK
    prod             dev/stg      FAIL  (symmetric)
    unset            prod         OK    (legacy prod-by-default: runners/coord)
    unset            dev/stg      FAIL  (non-prod DB without declaring non-prod)
    any              unknown-pg   FAIL  (fail-closed on unrecognised PG)
    any              sqlite/skip  OK    (legacy/tests/non-PG)
    ===============  ==========  =======
    """
    cls = classify_dsn(dsn)
    if cls in ("sqlite", "skip"):
        return

    canon_env = canonical_env(env if env is not None else os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_ENV"))
    redacted = _redact(dsn or "")

    # Break-glass: refused in prod (env OR DSN) so it can never silence a real
    # prod mismatch; valid prod states (unset+prod / prod+prod) never need it.
    if _truthy(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_ENV_CONTRACT_DISABLE")):
        if canon_env == "prod" or cls == "prod":
            raise EnvContractViolation(
                f"OMNISIGHT_ENV_CONTRACT_DISABLE=1 is refused when env or DSN is prod "
                f"(env={canon_env!r}, dsn-class={cls!r}, source={source}, dsn={redacted})."
            )
        _logger.warning(
            "env-contract DISABLED via OMNISIGHT_ENV_CONTRACT_DISABLE=1 "
            "(env=%r, dsn-class=%r, source=%s) — break-glass, non-prod only.",
            canon_env, cls, source,
        )
        return

    # Test/CI carveout: tests call init_pool/connect directly with assorted
    # DSNs. Bypass ONLY when not pointed at prod — a prod DSN inside a test is
    # still a real violation worth surfacing.
    in_test = bool(os.environ.get("PYTEST_CURRENT_TEST")) or _truthy(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_CI_MODE"))
    if in_test and cls != "prod":
        return

    # Unrecognised Postgres → hard fail (never fail-open).
    if cls == "unknown":
        raise EnvContractViolation(
            f"DSN resolves to an unrecognised Postgres database — refusing to connect "
            f"(env={canon_env!r}, source={source}, dsn={redacted}). Expected a db/user "
            f"matching dev (omnisight_dev), staging (*_staging) or prod (omnisight)."
        )

    # cls is one of dev/staging/prod from here.
    expected = "prod" if canon_env is None else canon_env
    if cls != expected:
        env_desc = "unset (legacy prod-by-default)" if canon_env is None else repr(canon_env)
        raise EnvContractViolation(
            f"env-contract violation: OMNISIGHT_ENV={env_desc} but the DSN looks like "
            f"'{cls}' (source={source}, dsn={redacted}). A '{expected}' process must use a "
            f"'{expected}' database. Set OMNISIGHT_ENV to match, or point at the correct DB."
        )


def _redact(dsn: str) -> str:
    """Mask the password in a DSN for safe logging."""

; systemd unit template for the OmniSight stateless agent worker (O3).
;
; Install:
;   sudo cp deploy/systemd/omnisight-worker@.service /etc/systemd/system/
;   # Edit USER_HOME / USERNAME placeholders below for your install.
;   sudo systemctl daemon-reload
;   sudo systemctl enable --now omnisight-worker@1
;   sudo systemctl enable --now omnisight-worker@2
;   ...
;
; The "@N" template lets the operator scale workers horizontally:
; ``omnisight-worker@1`` and ``omnisight-worker@2`` are independent
; processes drained from the same shared queue (O2).  Each one
; registers itself in the Redis ``workers:active`` set with TTL 90s
; and writes a heartbeat every 30s — see backend/worker.py.
;
; Tunables via the EnvironmentFile (.env at the project root):
;   * OMNISIGHT_REDIS_URL          — required for shared queue + locks.
;   * OMNISIGHT_WORKER_CAPACITY    — concurrent in-flight per worker.
;   * OMNISIGHT_WORKER_TENANT_FILTER     — comma-list of tenant_ids.
;   * OMNISIGHT_WORKER_CAPABILITY_FILTER — comma-list of agent caps.
;
; Graceful shutdown: systemd sends SIGTERM, the worker stops claiming
; new work, finishes in-flight tasks, releases its dist-locks, and
; deregisters from the active set.  TimeoutStopSec gives it 60s to
; finish before SIGKILL.

[Unit]
Description=OmniSight stateless agent worker (instance %i)
Documentation=https://github.com/your-org/OmniSight-Productizer
After=network-online.target docker.service
Wants=network-online.target
Requires=docker.service

    CAP=$${OMNISIGHT_WORKER_CAPACITY:-1}; \

    CAPS=$${OMNISIGHT_WORKER_CAPABILITY_FILTER:-}; \
    exec python3 -m backend.worker run \
        --worker-id "wkr-$$(hostname -s)-%i" \
        --capacity $$CAP \
        --tenant-filter "$$TENANTS" \
        --capability-filter "$$CAPS"'

docs/audit/codex-reviews/coordinator-acting-executor-ticket-draft-codex-audit-2026-05-23.txt:2479:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family6-image-db-drift-contract.md:122:4. **Exit 78 is semantically right.** `EX_CONFIG` (78) is the BSD `sysexits.h` code for "configuration error". Image-BEHIND-DB is a deploy-time misconfiguration: the operator selected an image incompatible with the DB. Other exit codes (1, 2, 70) would either be too generic or wrong; 78 communicates to systemd / docker-compose / kubelet that this is not transient (so don't restart) and not a program bug (so don't page on `up == 0` per `OmniSightBackendCrashloop`).
docs/audit/codex-reviews/coordinator-acting-executor-ticket-draft-codex-audit-2026-05-23.txt:2481:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family6-image-db-drift-contract.md:281:- **Does not gate `/readyz`.** The `/readyz` 503-with-`remediation` of §5 is the per-poll synchronous detector for systemd / docker-compose / kubelet's healthcheck. The gauge is the periodic observational detector for Prometheus / AlertBridge. They are independent.

docs/audit/codex-reviews/dag-execution-engine-audit-codex-2026-05-24.txt:2120:docs/audit/codex-reviews/a2-env-contract-guard-codex-audit-2026-05-24.txt:2621:./HANDOFF.md:28320:> **前回合（Phase-3 PG cutover — LIVE ON POSTGRESQL；兩段式交付：上半場 infra-ready 在 Step 5 reassessment 遇到 `backend/db.py` aiosqlite-hardcoded blocker 依 Production Readiness Gate SOP 停在 deployed-inactive checkpoint；使用者看了 checkpoint 後指示「可以接受暫時停機升級更新，請直接進行資料庫遷移」授權 downtime maintenance window → 下半場以 aiosqlite-compat wrapper over asyncpg（非全面 80 call site port）完成 runtime port + 真 cutover）**：使用者接續 Phase-1+2 後的三階段 plan 最後一階 G4 PG cutover，選 A「F1→F4 完整補齊 + Lessons Learned」路徑、明確要求 **「小心謹慎依照 SOP」**；Step 1 深度分析先挖到兩個 production-breaking gap（PG driver 從未進 production image + migrator 落後 7 張表），fixed as F1-F4 + 寫 Production Readiness Gate 進 SOP（commit `12a96388`）；Step 3 執行真 cutover dry-run 又挖到更深的結構裂縫：**`backend/db.py` 1592 行、80 個 `_conn()` 呼叫點、31 個 SQLite-specific SQL 還是 aiosqlite-only；`db_connection.py` 的 `AsyncDBConnection` 抽象沒有任何 production import**——G4 #2 shipped URL 解析 + driver dispatcher 但 runtime 接線零；flip `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL` 會被 db.py 靜默忽略（它只讀 `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_PATH`）；照 Production Readiness Gate SOP「dev-green ≠ prod-ready」約定**停在 deployed-inactive**、把 runtime port 作為新 Phase 排入 TODO.md。**做了什麼** — (a) **Sub-phase 3-A（PG HA pair live）**：`openssl rand -base64 32` 產密碼寫 `deploy/postgres-ha/.env`（gitignored）；primary 1x2s healthy 但 standby 進 restart loop —`"root" execution of the PostgreSQL server is not permitted`；root cause：`init-standby.sh` 用 entrypoint override 跑進 root、`exec postgres` 被 server security guard 擋；fix：mirror docker-entrypoint pattern 加 `chown -R postgres:postgres $PGDATA` + `exec gosu postgres postgres ...`（gosu bundled 在 postgres:16-alpine）；commit `f465051e`；`pg_stat_replication` 顯示 `omnisight_standby streaming async`. (b) **Sub-phase 3-B（backend ↔ pg-primary 網路）**：`docker-compose.prod.yml` declare `db_ha` external network + backend-a/b dual-home；hot-attach via `docker network connect` 不重啟；DNS `pg-primary` 從兩 replica 解得到；production image 內 `await asyncpg.connect(...)` live 打通 PG 16.13；commit `c0300fec`. (c) **Sub-phase 3-C 第一次 crash — 7 張整表 + 7 個 column alembic drift**：`alembic upgrade head` 後 migrate 立刻 `UndefinedColumnError workflow_runs.dag_plan_id`；發現 `backend/db.py::_migrate()` 在 SQLite 跑 44 個 ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN 沒有對應 alembic migration；live SQLite vs fresh PG column diff 挖出 `bootstrap_state / dag_plans / iq_runs / mfa_backup_codes / password_history / prompt_versions / user_mfa` 7 張整表 + `sessions.ua_hash / users.must_change_password / workflow_runs.{dag_plan_id,successor_run_id} / workflow_steps.dag_task_id / episodic_memory.{decayed_score,last_used_at}` 7 個 column 缺；F2 只鎖 table-level、alembic-vs-live column-level 是第三軸從沒守住。(d) **Sub-phase 3-C 第二次 crash — `event_log.id` 沒 IDENTITY**：`int(None)` on `reset_identity_sequences`；root cause：0001 baseline 寫 `INTEGER PRIMARY KEY` 沒 AUTOINCREMENT、`alembic_pg_compat._translate_autoincrement` 的 regex 只抓顯式 AUTOINCREMENT、PG 拿 plain int；app 的 `INSERT INTO event_log (event_type, ...)` 會 NOT NULL violation。(e) **Sub-phase 3-C 第三次 crash — `REAL` 精度截斷破 Merkle 鏈**：`exit 5 target_chain_ok=false first_bad_id=1 why=curr_hash`；per-row diff `audit_log.ts`：SQLite `1776547569.684335`、PG `1776547584.0` — **SQLite REAL binary64 (8-byte) vs PG REAL binary32 (4-byte)**、半精度截斷小數秒破 `sha256(prev_hash || canonical || str(round(ts,6)))`。(f) **P1 — alembic 0016 + shim + test 三件事一 commit**（`7d79e42e`、549 行）：`backend/alembic/versions/0016_pg_schema_sync.py` dialect-aware（PG 用 `IF NOT EXISTS` idempotent、SQLite 跳過 ALTER 避免 `_migrate()` 雙寫 duplicate column）+ `event_log.id` guarded `ADD GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY`（讀 `pg_attribute.attidentity` 判斷避免 double-ADD）；`alembic_pg_compat._translate_real` 加 `REAL → DOUBLE PRECISION`，DDL-only 預防（`_DDL_GUARD_RE` filter `CREATE TABLE`/`ALTER TABLE`/`ADD COLUMN` 避免參數字串誤傷）；`backend/tests/test_migrator_schema_coverage.py::test_alembic_pg_matches_sqlite_column_by_column` 跨 dialect column-parity 測試（`OMNI_TEST_PG_URL` env skip pattern、CI `pg-live-integration` job 會 exercise）；`DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE` 全量 re-upgrade 後所有 REAL 欄（30+ 欄）改 DOUBLE PRECISION、`migrate --truncate-target --json` **exit 0 / source_chain_ok=true / target_chain_ok=true / 1150 rows / 9 IDENTITY sequences reset**. (g) **Step 5 reassessment 發現真 blocker — backend/db.py 尚未 port**：`grep import aiosqlite` → 只 `db.py / tenant_secrets.py / db_connection.py / test_j5`；`grep from backend.db_connection` → 只 `db_connection.py / test_db_url`；**production code 零 import AsyncDBConnection**；結論：G4 marked `[x]` 時 runtime 接線為零、`[x]` 是「code merged」不是「prod runs」；照 Production Readiness Gate SOP 停 cutover、checkpoint 給 operator。**未動到的部分** — `backend/db.py` / `backend/tenant_secrets.py` / `backend/audit.py` / `backend/session_store.py` / 所有 production runtime code 0 diff（本 session 純 infra + alembic + 測試）；前端、Caddy、frontend、cloudflared 0 改動；**沒 flip `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL`**、沒 rolling restart 接 PG；prod 跟 Step 3 執行前 100% 一樣（SQLite）、`/readyz` 綠、audit_log 1115 rows 無損. **未做**（刻意、Production Readiness Gate SOP 停機約定）— cutover ceremony 沒開、T+5/T+10/T+30 監控沒跑（沒 cutover 可監控）、sqlite-matrix CI job 沒 archive、`backend/db.py` 80 個 call site 沒 touch. **設計考量** — (1) **停在 deployed-inactive 不硬衝**：Production Readiness Gate 明文「code in main + tests green + CI 通過 ≠ production runs」；硬 flip env 會 silent 沒效、operator 以為 cutover 其實沒；TODO.md A3 Phase 3 改拆成 3-Infra `[D]` + 3-Runtime `[ ]` 誠實反映. (2) **alembic 0016 dialect-aware ALTER TABLE**：SQLite 沒 `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`、dev SQLite 已被 `_migrate()` ALTER 過、再跑 duplicate error；PG 有 `IF NOT EXISTS` 9.6+ 支援；用 `conn.dialect.name == "postgresql"` 分流、SQLite 走 no-op. (3) **`REAL → DOUBLE PRECISION` shim rewrite 只在 DDL context**：regex `\bREAL\b` 全局替換理論上參數字串含 'REAL' 會誤傷；用 `_DDL_GUARD_RE` 預先 filter. (4) **nuke-and-replay 而不是寫 0017 retrofit**：PG 尚未承載 production 資料、`DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE` + re-upgrade 最乾淨、把 0001-0015 歷史 migration 都用修好的 shim 重跑一次；若未來 cutover 後才發現這類 shim bug 就只能 0017 retrofit. (5) **alembic 跑法 — production image + bind-mount**：既有 image 尚未 rebuild 含 0016 + shim 修；migration 是 offline DDL 不碰 runtime、bind-mount 迭代 low-risk；真 cutover 前要 rebuild baked-in. (6) **F3 column-parity test skip-by-default**：`OMNI_TEST_PG_URL` env gate 同 `test_alembic_pg_live_upgrade.py` pattern、本地 runner 不需 PG service container、CI job provision 好時 auto-exercise. (7) **為何 port 不在本 session**：80 call site per-site review SQLite-isms、一個 session 做完風險高於接受更長時程；Production Readiness Gate SOP 的 `[x] vs [D]` 分層本身就為防這種反 pattern. **驗收 / 自評** — (i) `git log --oneline 12a96388..HEAD` 顯示 3 commit（`f465051e` / `c0300fec` / `7d79e42e`）全 3-trailer. (ii) PG HA live + streaming：`docker ps` primary/standby healthy、`pg_stat_replication` streaming async. (iii) Migrator E2E 綠：exit 0 / chains 綠 / 1150 rows. (iv) Column-parity：SQLite ↔ PG diff CLEAN. (v) Prod 未受影響：`curl https://ai.sora-dev.app/api/v1/health` online、`backend-a /readyz` ready、SQLite 1115 audit_log rows 無損. (vi) SOP Step 1-5 綠、Step 6 是本 entry、Step 7 因 deployed-inactive 不跑 live demo. (vii) 新工作項 queued：TODO.md Phase 3-Runtime row 明文 1754 行 + 80 call site 估 2-3 session. **下一手要做的** — (A) **Phase 3-Runtime port（blocking cutover）**：`_conn()` 改 return `AsyncDBConnection`；80 call site 換 placeholder、處理 SQLite-isms、拆 row_factory 依賴；`tenant_secrets.py` 同步；PG matrix CI 全綠；建議分 session 做. (B) **真 cutover 後**：rebuild backend image 含 alembic 0016 + shim baked-in；`.env` flip；rolling restart；`docs/ops/db_failover.md` §4 ceremony；T+5/T+10/T+30 監控. (C) **G4 retrospective backfill**：TODO.md G4 row 1556 `[x]` 實為 `[x]+[D]-infra+[ ]-runtime`、建議拆 sub-bullet 顯示 gap；這也是 Production Readiness Gate SOP 的「舊 [x] row 不強制回填、新 row 必須使用新分層」實例，G4 是 transition case 可示範 good hygiene. (D) **F3 column-parity CI exercise**：等 `pg-live-integration` job 下次跑時首度 exercise、確認 cross-dialect diff clean. (E) **更激進選項（不建議）**：SQLAlchemy Core 薄 wrapper runtime 翻 `INSERT OR IGNORE` 等 SQLite-ism、hot path 放邏輯太多、不建議. **風險 / 已知問題** — (1) **G4 row 1556 `[x]` 歷史失真**：`[x]` 舊 SOP 語意是 merged、沒 enforce「prod 實跑」；其他 marked `[x]` row 可能也有 runtime 沒接但 infra 接了、建議 Phase 3-Runtime 完成後順便審 I9 / I10 / M3 / 其他 G 系列 row. (2) **alembic 0016 在 SQLite 側沒真跑過**：本 session 只 PG 跑；SQLite 現場 `alembic_version` 還在 0015、dialect-aware 邏輯理論上 SQLite no-op 但沒實測；建議 port 前在 dev SQLite 跑一次驗不破現有資料（尤其 bootstrap_state 有 rows 的表）. (3) **`_translate_real` regex 誤傷面**：已用 DDL guard 限制、極端 edge case 如 CREATE TABLE COMMENT 含 literal 'REAL' 會被改；pragmatic 接受. (4) **`event_log` IDENTITY retrofit 的 `pg_attribute.attidentity` check**：PG-specific API、future PG 版本升級（16→17/18）可能要重審該 query. (5) **PG HA pair 本地跑無 external monitoring**：`docker ps` 綠 ≠ 生產監控；真 cutover 前建議加 pg_exporter + Grafana + replication lag alert（`docs/ops/db_failover.md` §9.1 已列 cutover-後 follow-up）. (b) **Sub-phase 3-B（backend ↔ pg-primary 網路）**：`docker-compose.prod.yml` declare `db_ha` external network + backend-a/b dual-home；hot-attach via `docker network connect` 不重啟；DNS `pg-primary` 從兩 replica 解得到；production image 內 `await asyncpg.connect(...)` live 打通 PG 16.13；commit `c0300fec`. (c) **Sub-phase 3-C 第一次 crash — 7 張整表 + 7 個 column alembic drift**：`alembic upgrade head` 後 migrate 立刻 `UndefinedColumnError workflow_runs.dag_plan_id`；發現 `backend/db.py::_migrate()` 在 SQLite 跑 44 個 ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN 沒有對應 alembic migration；live SQLite vs fresh PG column diff 挖出 `bootstrap_state / dag_plans / iq_runs / mfa_backup_codes / password_history / prompt_versions / user_mfa` 7 張整表 + `sessions.ua_hash / users.must_change_password / workflow_runs.{dag_plan_id,successor_run_id} / workflow_steps.dag_task_id / episodic_memory.{decayed_score,last_used_at}` 7 個 column 缺；F2 只鎖 table-level、alembic-vs-live column-level 是第三軸從沒守住。(d) **Sub-phase 3-C 第二次 crash — `event_log.id` 沒 IDENTITY**：`int(None)` on `reset_identity_sequences`；root cause：0001 baseline 寫 `INTEGER PRIMARY KEY` 沒 AUTOINCREMENT、`alembic_pg_compat._translate_autoincrement` 的 regex 只抓顯式 AUTOINCREMENT、PG 拿 plain int；app 的 `INSERT INTO event_log (event_type, ...)` 會 NOT NULL violation。(e) **Sub-phase 3-C 第三次 crash — `REAL` 精度截斷破 Merkle 鏈**：`exit 5 target_chain_ok=false first_bad_id=1 why=curr_hash`；per-row diff `audit_log.ts`：SQLite `1776547569.684335`、PG `1776547584.0` — **SQLite REAL binary64 (8-byte) vs PG REAL binary32 (4-byte)**、半精度截斷小數秒破 `sha256(prev_hash || canonical || str(round(ts,6)))`。(f) **P1 — alembic 0016 + shim + test 三件事一 commit**（`7d79e42e`、549 行）：`backend/alembic/versions/0016_pg_schema_sync.py` dialect-aware（PG 用 `IF NOT EXISTS` idempotent、SQLite 跳過 ALTER 避免 `_migrate()` 雙寫 duplicate column）+ `event_log.id` guarded `ADD GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY`（讀 `pg_attribute.attidentity` 判斷避免 double-ADD）；`alembic_pg_compat._translate_real` 加 `REAL → DOUBLE PRECISION`，DDL-only 預防（`_DDL_GUARD_RE` filter `CREATE TABLE`/`ALTER TABLE`/`ADD COLUMN` 避免參數字串誤傷）；`backend/tests/test_migrator_schema_coverage.py::test_alembic_pg_matches_sqlite_column_by_column` 跨 dialect column-parity 測試（`OMNI_TEST_PG_URL` env skip pattern、CI `pg-live-integration` job 會 exercise）；`DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE` 全量 re-upgrade 後所有 REAL 欄（30+ 欄）改 DOUBLE PRECISION、`migrate --truncate-target --json` **exit 0 / source_chain_ok=true / target_chain_ok=true / 1150 rows / 9 IDENTITY sequences reset**. (g) **Step 5 reassessment 發現真 blocker — backend/db.py 尚未 port**：`grep import aiosqlite` → 只 `db.py / tenant_secrets.py / db_connection.py / test_j5`；`grep from backend.db_connection` → 只 `db_connection.py / test_db_url`；**production code 零 import AsyncDBConnection**；結論：G4 marked `[x]` 時 runtime 接線為零、`[x]` 是「code merged」不是「prod runs」；照 Production Readiness Gate SOP 停 cutover、checkpoint 給 operator。**未動到的部分** — `backend/db.py` / `backend/tenant_secrets.py` / `backend/audit.py` / `backend/session_store.py` / 所有 production runtime code 0 diff（本 session 純 infra + alembic + 測試）；前端、Caddy、frontend、cloudflared 0 改動；**沒 flip `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL`**、沒 rolling restart 接 PG；prod 跟 Step 3 執行前 100% 一樣（SQLite）、`/readyz` 綠、audit_log 1115 rows 無損. **未做**（刻意、Production Readiness Gate SOP 停機約定）— cutover ceremony 沒開、T+5/T+10/T+30 監控沒跑（沒 cutover 可監控）、sqlite-matrix CI job 沒 archive、`backend/db.py` 80 個 call site 沒 touch. **設計考量** — (1) **停在 deployed-inactive 不硬衝**：Production Readiness Gate 明文「code in main + tests green + CI 通過 ≠ production runs」；硬 flip env 會 silent 沒效、operator 以為 cutover 其實沒；TODO.md A3 Phase 3 改拆成 3-Infra `[D]` + 3-Runtime `[ ]` 誠實反映. (2) **alembic 0016 dialect-aware ALTER TABLE**：SQLite 沒 `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`、dev SQLite 已被 `_migrate()` ALTER 過、再跑 duplicate error；PG 有 `IF NOT EXISTS` 9.6+ 支援；用 `conn.dialect.name == "postgresql"` 分流、SQLite 走 no-op. (3) **`REAL → DOUBLE PRECISION` shim rewrite 只在 DDL context**：regex `\bREAL\b` 全局替換理論上參數字串含 'REAL' 會誤傷；用 `_DDL_GUARD_RE` 預先 filter. (4) **nuke-and-replay 而不是寫 0017 retrofit**：PG 尚未承載 production 資料、`DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE` + re-upgrade 最乾淨、把 0001-0015 歷史 migration 都用修好的 shim 重跑一次；若未來 cutover 後才發現這類 shim bug 就只能 0017 retrofit. (5) **alembic 跑法 — production image + bind-mount**：既有 image 尚未 rebuild 含 0016 + shim 修；migration 是 offline DDL 不碰 runtime、bind-mount 迭代 low-risk；真 cutover 前要 rebuild baked-in. (6) **F3 column-parity test skip-by-default**：`OMNI_TEST_PG_URL` env gate 同 `test_alembic_pg_live_upgrade.py` pattern、本地 runner 不需 PG service container、CI job provision 好時 auto-exercise. (7) **為何 port 不在本 session**：80 call site per-site review SQLite-isms、一個 session 做完風險高於接受更長時程；Production Readiness Gate SOP 的 `[x] vs [D]` 分層本身就為防這種反 pattern. **驗收 / 自評** — (i) `git log --oneline 12a96388..HEAD` 顯示 3 commit（`f465051e` / `c0300fec` / `7d79e42e`）全 3-trailer. (ii) PG HA live + streaming：`docker ps` primary/standby healthy、`pg_stat_replication` streaming async. (iii) Migrator E2E 綠：exit 0 / chains 綠 / 1150 rows. (iv) Column-parity：SQLite ↔ PG diff CLEAN. (v) Prod 未受影響：`curl https://ai.sora-dev.app/api/v1/health` online、`backend-a /readyz` ready、SQLite 1115 audit_log rows 無損. (vi) SOP Step 1-5 綠、Step 6 是本 entry、Step 7 因 deployed-inactive 不跑 live demo. (vii) 新工作項 queued：TODO.md Phase 3-Runtime row 明文 1754 行 + 80 call site 估 2-3 session. **下一手要做的** — (A) **Phase 3-Runtime port（blocking cutover）**：`_conn()` 改 return `AsyncDBConnection`；80 call site 換 placeholder、處理 SQLite-isms、拆 row_factory 依賴；`tenant_secrets.py` 同步；PG matrix CI 全綠；建議分 session 做. (B) **真 cutover 後**：rebuild backend image 含 alembic 0016 + shim baked-in；`.env` flip；rolling restart；`docs/ops/db_failover.md` §4 ceremony；T+5/T+10/T+30 監控. (C) **G4 retrospective backfill**：TODO.md G4 row 1556 `[x]` 實為 `[x]+[D]-infra+[ ]-runtime`、建議拆 sub-bullet 顯示 gap；這也是 Production Readiness Gate SOP 的「舊 [x] row 不強制回填、新 row 必須使用新分層」實例，G4 是 transition case 可示範 good hygiene. (D) **F3 column-parity CI exercise**：等 `pg-live-integration` job 下次跑時首度 exercise、確認 cross-dialect diff clean. (E) **更激進選項（不建議）**：SQLAlchemy Core 薄 wrapper runtime 翻 `INSERT OR IGNORE` 等 SQLite-ism、hot path 放邏輯太多、不建議. **風險 / 已知問題** — (1) **G4 row 1556 `[x]` 歷史失真**：`[x]` 舊 SOP 語意是 merged、沒 enforce「prod 實跑」；其他 marked `[x]` row 可能也有 runtime 沒接但 infra 接了、建議 Phase 3-Runtime 完成後順便審 I9 / I10 / M3 / 其他 G 系列 row. (2) **alembic 0016 在 SQLite 側沒真跑過**：本 session 只 PG 跑；SQLite 現場 `alembic_version` 還在 0015、dialect-aware 邏輯理論上 SQLite no-op 但沒實測；建議 port 前在 dev SQLite 跑一次驗不破現有資料（尤其 bootstrap_state 有 rows 的表）. (3) **`_translate_real` regex 誤傷面**：已用 DDL guard 限制、極端 edge case 如 CREATE TABLE COMMENT 含 literal 'REAL' 會被改；pragmatic 接受. (4) **`event_log` IDENTITY retrofit 的 `pg_attribute.attidentity` check**：PG-specific API、future PG 版本升級（16→17/18）可能要重審該 query. (5) **PG HA pair 本地跑無 external monitoring**：`docker ps` 綠 ≠ 生產監控；真 cutover 前建議加 pg_exporter + Grafana + replication lag alert（`docs/ops/db_failover.md` §9.1 已列 cutover-後 follow-up）. 再吃，pytest.ini 加 95% gate 說明（opt-in CLI、不進 addopts 避免個別 file 跑爆）+ plan-doc baseline snapshot。**未動到的部分 / 刻意 scope 紀律**：Epic 9 (#82) 多 worker concurrency test suite 擴展（multi_worker harness skeleton 已落、library 擴張留到後續；skeleton 已擋住 4 個真 bug 就是價值）；Epic 11 (#85/#70) operator deploy + 24h 觀察窗留到下一 session；`auth` / `db` coverage 剩餘 gap；`test_migrator_schema_coverage.py` SQLite drift-guard 刻意留（by design，and `backend.db` 95% gate 的 option C 是 carve 出來留 75% guard rail — 未決）；`sessions`/`users`/`audit_log` RLS domain tests 分散在 `backend/tests/test_db_*.py`（legacy `tests/test_rls.py` 已遷）；CI shard 4-way aggregate 仍 60% gate（提升 95% 需 Epic 10.4 full sweep，option A 不含）。**未做**（刻意）：沒重寫 Epic 1-5 的既有 commit history（tech-debt 不 rewrite 是紀律）；沒 touch frontend code（Phase-3-v2 純 backend 重構、API contract 零破）；沒動 alembic 0001-0016（往前 migrations 已驗過、不回頭動）；沒加新 alembic 除了 0017 (tsvector) + 0018 (mfa_challenges)；沒 touch `backend.db` SQLite `_SCHEMA` + `_migrate` dev-mode 區塊（post-C.2 已 dead on prod、local dev 還要用、刪除成本高 gain 低）；沒重新 calibrate rate limit per_user / per_tenant（只動 per_ip 對 dashboard 友善、per_user 抑濫用上 shared-IP 場景是獨立議題）；沒遷 `tests/test_mfa.py` + `tests/test_api_keys.py` + `tests/test_tenant_fs.py` 等其他 legacy fixture-pattern test（blast 是 file-scoped、獨立 ticket）；沒升 `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL` 的 parse/validation（`_resolve_pg_dsn` 接受的 prefix 集合不變）。**設計考量** — (1) **為何 polymorphic conn 而非 explicit pool-everywhere**：`async def fn(conn=None)` + `if conn is None: async with get_pool().acquire() as owned_conn: return await _impl(owned_conn, ...)` 的 pattern 讓 caller 選 — 單次操作讓 helper 借 pool，多語句 atomicity 讓 caller 開 `async with conn.transaction()` 然後把 conn 傳進來，一致 interface、tx 邊界交給 caller 但 default 仍安全。(2) **為何 `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))` 而非 row-level `SELECT FOR UPDATE`**：audit hash chain、session rotation、secret upsert 這類「同 key 序列化但不要全表鎖」的場合，advisory lock 是 app-level、跟 MVCC 讀者完全不相干、scope 到 tx commit 自動釋放、比 SELECT FOR UPDATE 更輕（不鎖 row、不阻塞讀）；hashtext() 確保 TEXT key 能塞進 64-bit lock space。(3) **為何 `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE ... RETURNING id` 比 SELECT+INSERT 安全**：atomicity — 兩 worker 同時做 SELECT 都看不到 row → 同時 INSERT、後者走 ON CONFLICT 路徑 UPDATE 現有 row、RETURNING 讀回 pre-existing id、**不**拋 UniqueViolation；這是 tenant_secrets SP-4.6 發現並 fix 的真 bug，舊 SELECT-then-INSERT 在 pool 並發下會撞。(4) **為何 `multiprocessing.spawn` 而非 fork**：asyncpg + uvloop + SSL context 不跨 fork 乾淨（stale FD、shared connection state）；spawn re-import parent module in child 匹配 `uvicorn --workers N` 真實 lifecycle、每個 worker 自己的 module-globals 全 fresh、抓到 fork 藏起來的 shared-state bug（`_webauthn_challenges` / `_pending_mfa` / `_api_keys_legacy` / `auth_baseline_mode` 都是這樣被抓到）。(5) **為何 xfail(strict=True)**：Step B 每顆 fix 前先寫 regression test + 打 `xfail(strict=True)` marker、pre-fix 預期 fail 回 XFAIL (pytest 視為 pass)；post-fix test 通、strict 把 XPASS flip 成 FAIL 逼 fix-commit 一起刪 marker；這樣 fix author 不可能「只改 code 不刪 marker」留下 silent pass；4 顆 B 都這路走、都按 spec 刪 marker。(6) **為何 `.env.test` 隔離**：operator 跑 test 前不必清 dev `.env` 的 `OMNISIGHT_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`、fresh-install assumption 不被真 key 破；`OMNISIGHT_DOTENV_FILE` env var 指向 test 專用 file（無 provider key、無 tenant override）、conftest 也 scrub 幾個 `OMNISIGHT_*` env defense-in-depth。(7) **為何 subprocess 測 `_cli_main` 拿 coverage**：`asyncio.run()` 嵌在已 running loop（pytest-asyncio）crash；subprocess = 全新 loop、pytest-cov `COVERAGE_PROCESS_START` + sitecustomize 自動開 `.coverage.<pid>`、combine 吸回；honest 測的是真 CLI invocation shape、coverage 精準。(8) **為何 plan-reorder**：原 plan Epic 6 「test-file sweep 4 SP」在 Epic 5 收尾時觀察到很多 test 因 A/B 本身完成而 auto-resolve，residual 只剩 ~5-10 file；reclaim 的 budget 搬去 Step A (known-missing work) + Step B (4 個有 spec 的真 concurrency bug 都配 xfail strict guard)；total commit count 不變、delivered concurrency-correctness 增量明顯提升。**驗收 / 自評** — (i) `git log --since='2026-04-20 10:00' --oneline` 75 commits 全 3-trailer（env user + global user + Claude 1M）。(ii) `alembic heads` = `0018`、`alembic check` green、`alembic upgrade head` 在 fresh test PG 從零一路跑到 0018 成功。(iii) `backend/tests/test_epic6_prep_multi_worker_state.py` 3/3 綠（B.1~B.4 四個 fix 驗完、multi_worker harness 跑 subprocess 真穿過 xfail→pass flip）。(iv) Coverage gate: `db_context` + `db_pool` 100%、`tenant_secrets` 98%、`audit` 99%、aggregate 71%（從 baseline 63% 提升），`auth` 65% + `db` 55% 兩塊 PENDING。(v) `grep "db\._conn("` 只剩 docstring mentions + `test_migrator_schema_coverage.py` 的 SQLite drift-guard（by design），prod code 零 `_conn()` live call。(vi) `git rm backend/db_pg_compat.py` 後 `from backend import db, main, db_pool` 零 ImportError。(vii) Regression sweep（898/903 relevant tests, 550/555 mfa-tenant-auth slice）綠、剩餘 5 failures 全 pre-existing（`test_container_tenant_budget × 3` SQLite-fixture pool-init regression、`test_recovery::TestErrorBoundary` React 頁被中文化、`test_alembic_0017_tsvector::TestMigrationDowngrade` STORED-column-drop 與 pg_test_conn savepoint race — 驗證 at pre-C.2 HEAD 同樣 reproduces、非本輪引入）。**下一手要做的** — (A) **#82 Epic 9 concurrency tests 擴張**（multi_worker harness library — pool concurrency 50-task stress / multi-tenant isolation 20-parallel / tx boundary 四 scenario / pool exhaustion + 503 shape / SSE scope-reality-check）：skeleton for audit_log)、build INSERT（tenants 走 ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING 吸收 Alembic 0012 seed）、`executemany` 批次、row count 對齊（不對齊 exit 6）、最後 `verify_target_chain()` 對 PG 再跑一次 re-walk broken→exit 5（意味搬移 bug）。**Exit code table**：0 成功 / 1 unexpected（FileNotFoundError / asyncpg 沒裝） / 2 CLI usage（malformed URL / missing target） / 3 source chain broken（絕不寫 target） / 4 target dirty 需 --truncate-target / 5 target chain broken 搬移 bug / 6 row count 不一致。(2) `backend/tests/test_migrate_sqlite_to_pg.py`（51 顆契約 7 class × 0.99 s）：**TestScriptShape 6**（檔案存在 / --help subprocess 乾淨 / TABLES_IN_ORDER 非空 / tenants ASCII payload / per-tenant genesis prev_hash="" / canonical JSON 排序+無空白 / recompute_hash determinism）、**TestSqlBuilders 7**（basic INSERT / quoted identifiers 防 "user" PG keyword collision / ON psycopg2 / target 拒 SQLite / 9 旗標 wire / subprocess exit 2 on bad URL / missing target without dry-run / missing source entirely）、**TestDryRunE2E 4**（實 SQLite fixture DB valid chain → exit 0 + seeded tenants 1 row 容忍 / id ASC 插入順序保留 / sequences_reset audit_log=5 / broken source 0 writes to target / TRUNCATE 先發射 / --tables filter 尊重 / unknown table 拋 ValueError）、**TestReportShape 2**（MigrationReport + TableResult to_dict()）；**零**修改 `backend/db.py` / `backend/db_url.py` / `backend/db_connection.py` / `backend/audit.py` / `backend/alembic_pg_compat.py` / `backend/alembic/env.py` / `deploy/postgres-ha/` / compose / routing / server / frontend / requirements.txt / requirements-lock.hash 一行；零新 Python 依賴（asyncpg + aiosqlite 皆 lazy import，G4 #2 已用）。**為什麼不用 pgloader / sqlite3-to-postgres PyPI** — 它們以 PK order 重排 row 並重寫型別（TEXT→VARCHAR 等），audit_log 的 `prev_hash→curr_hash` Merkle 鏈就會靜默毀掉整串 tamper-evidence，downstream 合規審計永久失效。**為什麼 pre+post 雙邊 chain verify** — pre 保護生產資料（source broken 絕不寫 target，避免把壞鏈帶進 PG），post 保護搬移器本身（若未來改了 canonical_json / round(ts, 6) / sha256 輸入順序，script 與 `backend.audit._hash` 產生實作漂移時 exit 5 會立刻 scream 而不是靜默生產壞鏈）。**為什麼 `pg_get_serial_sequence` 而不是硬寫 sequence 名** — PG 10+ IDENTITY 跟 legacy SERIAL 的 sequence 命名規則不同（`tablename_id_seq` vs auto），`pg_get_serial_sequence('audit_log', 'id')` 對兩者都通用。**為什麼 tenants 走 ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING 而不直接要求 TRUNCATE** — Alembic 0012 `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO tenants VALUES ('t-default', ...)` 是 schema bootstrap 必經步驟，operator 跑 `alembic upgrade head` 後再跑 migrate script 是標準流程；要求 TRUNCATE tenants 會逼 operator 在每次跑前手動 seed，不符 one-shot 契約；其他表仍嚴格走 `--truncate-target` gate 確保可重複執行不 dirty。**為什麼 `ORDER BY id ASC` 而不 `ORDER BY ts`** — SQLite 的 AUTOINCREMENT 保證 id 嚴格遞增，但兩筆 `time.time()` 呼叫在同一 ms 可能拿到一樣的 ts（timer resolution）；hash chain 依賴**插入順序**不是時戳順序，所以 `id ASC` 才是 audit.py 的 canonical sort。**為什麼 `executemany` 不是 COPY FROM** — COPY FROM STDIN 吞吐更高但跳過 INSERT 的 ON CONFLICT 機制（tenants 需要）+ 每 row 型別檢查（audit_log TEXT/REAL 混合 + tenant_id 預設值需對齊），correctness > throughput 對這個 one-shot 場景合理。**活體驗證** — (a) 51 條 V2 #6 契約測試本機 0.99 s 全綠、(b) G4 合計 479 顆全綠 8.49 s zero regression（`test_migrate_sqlite_to_pg 51 + test_db_url 75 + test_alembic_pg_compat 226 + test_pg_ha_deployment 114 + test_audit 13`）、(c) 額外 broader 回歸 `test_db 13 + test_bootstrap + test_auth + test_s0_sessions` 合計 66 顆全綠 13 s zero regression、(d) `python3 scripts/migrate_sqlite_to_pg.py --source data/omnisight.db --dry-run --json` 對**既有 dev SQLite DB** 跑通（exit 0、source_chain_ok=true、t-default tenant chain 綠、25 張業務表偵測完整）、(e) **不跑真 PG container**（G4 #5 CI Postgres service matrix 會接力跑 end-to-end live migration 驗證），本回合只鎖 pure function 契約 + async orchestrator via mock、(f) `python3 scripts/migrate_sqlite_to_pg.py --help` subprocess smoke 綠。**關鍵設計決策** — (a) Pure function first — `verify_hash_chain_in_rows` / `build_insert_sql` / `build_sequence_reset_sql` / `parse_source_arg` / `parse_target_arg` / `recompute_hash` / `canonical_json` / `canonical_payload` 皆零 I/O 零連線 seam，51 顆測試其中 37 顆走 pure function 路徑、14 顆走 subprocess + mock PG 路徑；(b) Source chain broken → 絕不 open PG 連線 → 絕不寫 target，exit 3 是 data safety invariant；(c) Target chain broken → exit 5 是 migration bug invariant（source OK + 搬移後不 OK = 搬移器錯）；(d) IDENTITY sequence reset 在 row copy 後才跑，否則 setval 到 MAX(id)=NULL 會爆；(e) FK-safe 順序 `tenants` 必首 — `api_keys` / `tenant_secrets` / `tenant_egress_*` 在 PG 走 REFERENCES tenants(id)，亂序會撞 FK；(f) asyncpg $N paramstyle 不 %s — 直接用 asyncpg native 避免 psycopg2 二轉；(g) `--dry-run` 不開 PG 連線 — pre-flight hash chain walk + 報表可在沒 PG 環境下先跑確認；(h) `--json` 輸出吃單行 JSON doc 適合 CI `jq .exit_code` 門檻；(i) `_FakePgConn` class-level stub 讓 51 顆 CI 測試不 install asyncpg — asyncpg 只 gated 在未來 G4 #5 live integration 測試；(j) 零新依賴讓 requirements-lock.hash 不變 = 零 CI churn；(k) 腳本放 `scripts/` 與 `alembic_dual_track.py` / `pg_ha_verify.py` / `scan_sqlite_isms.py` 同層，G4 ops 工具自成一族；(l) Cross-table 完整 row count 對齊驗證作為最後門檻 — 任何 silent drop exit 6。**Scope 自律** — 只新增 `scripts/migrate_sqlite_to_pg.py` + `backend/tests/test_migrate_sqlite_to_pg.py` 2 檔 + TODO row 1363 `[ ]`→`[x]` + 本 HANDOFF 條目；**不動** `backend/db.py` / `backend/db_url.py` / `backend/db_connection.py` / `backend/audit.py` / `backend/alembic_pg_compat.py` / `backend/alembic/env.py` / `deploy/postgres-ha/` / compose / Caddy / reverse-proxy / systemd / observability stack / requirements.in / requirements.txt / requirements-lock.hash / CLAUDE.md / SOP。**下一組可以開的工作項（G4 #5-6）** — row 1364 CI 新增 Postgres service matrix（把 G4 #3 docker-compose.yml + G4 #1 alembic_dual_track.py + G4 #4 migrate_sqlite_to_pg.py 三件套餵 CI），row 1365 交付 `docs/ops/db_failover.md` runbook（sync/async policy 決策表 synchronous_standby_names 打開切 sync replication），(8) `scripts/pg_ha_verify.py`（~350 行純 stdlib，零第三方 import：`parse_pg_conf` state-machine + `parse_hba` + 7 組 `backend/tests/test_pg_ha_deployment.py`（114 顆 × 11 class 契約測試 0.33 s）；**零**修改 `backend/db.py` / `backend/db_url.py` / `backend/db_connection.py` / `backend/config.py` / `backend/alembic_pg_compat.py` / `backend/alembic/env.py` / 既有 compose / Caddy / 任何 runtime 檔；零新 Python 依賴（scripts/pg_ha_verify.py 是 stdlib only）。**為什麼 sync 是 operator opt-in 不是預設** — sync 加 2-5 ms p50 latency（本地網路）或 20-50 ms p50（跨 AZ）對 OmniSight 大部分 audit_log / decision_log write path 不划算；留給 operator 按 compliance window 自行打開。G4 #3 checkbox 規範「synchronous_commit=on 可設」的意義不是硬開，而是**讓 operator 能一行 config edit + pg_ctl reload 開關**，這正是我們做的 — primary conf 顯式宣告 `synchronous_commit = on`（primary-side durability）+ `synchronous_standby_names = ''`（replication mode 切換鍵），配合 .env.example 的 operator guide block 三處 documented entry point。**為什麼 pg_hba 用 scram-sha-256 全線** — md5 從 PG 14 deprecated，wire 上 leak password hash；scram-sha-256 是 PG 14+ default + `primary_conninfo` 認證等同 channel。**為什麼用 named physical slot 不只靠 wal_keep_size** — slot 保證 primary 等 standby consumed WAL 才釋放即便 segment 到了 1 GB；wal_keep_size 只是 best-effort retention window，長斷線會觸發 re-basebackup。**為什麼 init-standby 每次 boot 重寫 postgresql.auto.conf** — 密碼輪替 operator 更新 `.env` + `docker compose restart pg-standby` 就生效，不需 `docker exec` 進 container；`chmod 0600` 確保 host 上即使被誤 cat 至少 owner-only。**為什麼 standby override container entrypoint 不走 initdb.d** — docker-entrypoint.sh 會先 `initdb` 空 PGDATA 然後跑 initdb.d；我們要**反過來**先 pg_basebackup 再 signal，所以只能覆寫 entrypoint 用自己的 init-standby.sh 接手。**為什麼刻意不把 primary_conninfo 塞進 postgresql.standby.conf** — 該檔 commit 進 git，塞密碼 = leak；runtime 寫入 postgresql.auto.conf 才是 PG 16 正確模式。**契約測試 114 條 0.33 s 分布** — (1) deploy directory shape 9、(2) primary conf 12、(3) standby conf 8（含「no primary_conninfo 在 static conf」用 parsed-dict 檢查）、(4) pg_hba 6、(5) compose 16（含 hba bind-mount 出現≥2 次因雙 service + primary config_file flag + passwords fail-closed）、(6) init-primary 10、(7) init-standby 15、(8) env example 14、(9) parse_pg_conf 8、(10) parse_hba 3、(11) verifier end-to-end 5（真跑 `python3 scripts/pg_ha_verify.py` 兩次 human+json + **negative gates 3**：砍 primary wal_level → exit 1、加 md5 row → exit 1、加 remote trust row → exit 1）、(12) symmetry 5（slot name 'omnisight_standby_slot' 四處 / application_name 'omnisight_standby' 四處含 primary conf comment / replicator user 三處 / postgres:16-alpine / sync-commit 三處 documentation chain — 解決「primary 寫 slot X 但 standby 用 slot Y」silent mis-routing）。**活體驗證** — (a) `python3 scripts/pg_ha_verify.py` 75/75 checks OK；(b) `python3 -m pytest backend/tests/test_pg_ha_deployment.py -q` → docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family8-graceful-shutdown-contract.md:344:## §5. `docker-compose.prod.yml` contract — `stop_grace_period` per service

This is what tells the docker daemon how long to wait between SIGTERM and SIGKILL when *something other than* the §3 script invokes `docker stop` — for example, a direct `docker compose down` from an operator's terminal, or the daemon's own shutdown sequence.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family8-graceful-shutdown-contract.md:350:The downstream ticket `v2-⑧-2b` adds these to `docker-compose.prod.yml`. The contract pins them exactly:
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family8-graceful-shutdown-contract.md:399:| Operator running `docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml down` from terminal | no `-t` passed | yes — uses `stop_grace_period` |
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family8-graceful-shutdown-contract.md:640:5. **`docker compose down` from operator terminal** — operator runs `docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml down` directly, bypassing the §3 script; verify the per-container `stop_grace_period` from §5 is what the daemon uses; verify no service is SIGKILLed prematurely (specifically: PG must get its full 30 s, backend its full 40 s).
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family8-graceful-shutdown-contract.md:643:8. **`stop_grace_period` cross-check** — programmatic check that every `-t N` value in `scripts/shutdown.sh` has a matching `stop_grace_period: Ns` for the same service in `docker-compose.prod.yml`; fail the test if they diverge.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family8-graceful-shutdown-contract.md:735:2. **Q12.1.2 — Does the `stop_grace_period` set in `docker-compose.prod.yml` need to be ALSO set on the matching `docker-compose.staging.yml`?** *Recommended at `v2-⑧-2b`:* yes, identical values, so staging exercises the same shutdown sequence pre-prod. The contract pins prod values; staging mirrors.

docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family6-image-db-drift-contract.md:62:4. There was no rescue path with operator-authority audit. The recovery the operator performed (overrides to `/livez` in 3 docker-compose lines + 3 Caddyfile lines) was correct but un-audited.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family6-image-db-drift-contract.md:122:4. **Exit 78 is semantically right.** `EX_CONFIG` (78) is the BSD `sysexits.h` code for "configuration error". Image-BEHIND-DB is a deploy-time misconfiguration: the operator selected an image incompatible with the DB. Other exit codes (1, 2, 70) would either be too generic or wrong; 78 communicates to systemd / docker-compose / kubelet that this is not transient (so don't restart) and not a program bug (so don't page on `up == 0` per `OmniSightBackendCrashloop`).
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family6-image-db-drift-contract.md:204:1. **Polling-coupled.** `/readyz`'s migration probe runs once per healthcheck poll (every 30 s in `docker-compose.prod.yml`). Between polls the drift is undetected. The 118 failing healthchecks the retro identifies were detected — but only at poll cadence; if the operator looked between polls, the same drift was momentarily invisible.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family6-image-db-drift-contract.md:206:3. **Hidden when readyz is disabled.** During the 2026-05-14 recovery, the operator swapped healthchecks from `/readyz` to `/livez` (3 docker-compose lines + 3 Caddyfile lines per the retro). Once healthchecks point at `/livez`, the migration probe never runs. **Detection went to zero**. An out-of-band gauge cannot be disabled by an in-band healthcheck swap.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family6-image-db-drift-contract.md:281:- **Does not gate `/readyz`.** The `/readyz` 503-with-`remediation` of §5 is the per-poll synchronous detector for systemd / docker-compose / kubelet's healthcheck. The gauge is the periodic observational detector for Prometheus / AlertBridge. They are independent.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family6-image-db-drift-contract.md:528:- **Image rollback.** Image management is a docker-compose / GHCR concern; the rescue CLI does not pull / tag / push images.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family6-image-db-drift-contract.md:632:3. **The build is the latest enforcement point.** CI runs lint and tests; both can be bypassed (`--no-verify`, skipping tests locally). The image build is gated behind the docker-compose / GHCR push step that operators DO trust; failing the build there is the latest non-bypassable signal.

**Verdict: NEEDS REVISION**

The split is directionally sound, but not ready to file. Main fixes: merge two tiny P0 tickets, split/retier host-action tickets, rewrite disabled-executor ACs so “Go-Live” means “shipped inert + locally exercised,” and add hard `MUST NOT` scope boundaries.

**Open Questions**
1. **P0-1 + P0-2**: merge. They are tiny, additive workflow/DAG data-model readiness edits with shared test surface. Call it `P0-A dag data-model executor readiness: failed transition + dag_task_id`.
2. **P1-3..P1-5**: keep split. Scheduler, handlers, and terminal/step wiring are distinct failure modes. Merging them creates one oversized “executor core” ticket and invites overreach.
3. **Disabled Phase-1 AC honesty**: “merged but disabled” is honest only if Deploy/Go-Live explicitly say “artifact/config shipped, default off, no daemon enabled.” Exercised must be local harness/unit/integration evidence, not live dev/staging.
4. **Runner-pickability**: P0-3, P1-1, P1-6, P1-9 have hidden host/live-env wording. P1-9 as written is not agent:auto. Split it.
5. **Dep graph**: mostly acyclic and sensible, but only if filed as real JIRA Blocks links. Text `blockedBy:` is not enough. P1-6 correctly blocks P1-9. Add P1-1/P1-2/P1-5/P1-6/P1-8 before any live enable.

**Must-Fix Filing Mechanics**
- `scripts/file_jira_ticket.py` always files **issuetype Story**. The draft’s `type:bug/feature/docs/meta` is only a label via `--type`; that is okay.
- Any ticket touching docs must include `area:docs`. P1-1 currently mentions cold-start inventory docs but areas omit docs.
- P1-10 touches `TODO.md`; Codex Tier B rules say do not modify `TODO.md`. Remove `TODO.md` from runner-visible scope or make that part human/Claude/tier:X.
- Do not use “migrated on dev/staging” in agent:auto AC. Runners can create/test migration files, not perform host migrations.

**Per-Ticket Changes**
| Ticket | Verdict | Required Change |
|---|---|---|
| EPIC | Keep | tier:X/meta OK. Children block it. |
| P0-1 | Merge | Merge with P0-2. |
| P0-2 | Merge | Merge with P0-1. |
| P0-3 | Keep, AC rewrite | Agent can add alembic + tests, not migrate dev host. Go-Live must be “merged; release migration pending normal deploy.” |
| P1-1 | Keep, tighten | Agent may add module + unit file only. Add `area:docs` only if editing inventory doc. No systemctl/install. |
| P1-2 | Keep | Good, but add explicit “do not import/use runner_coordination.” |
| P1-3 | Keep | Good. “Serial only; no concurrency/retry/crash-resume.” |
| P1-4 | Keep | High drift risk. Must forbid Gerrit/SSH/registry/hardware/cross-compile. |
| P1-5 | Keep | Good. Ensure no billing/export behavior changes here. |
| P1-6 | Keep, AC rewrite | Do not require real staging creds. Test with fake env/creds. Blocks P1-9. |
| P1-7 | Narrow or split | Either narrow to `finetune_export` only, or add files/tests for skills/billing. Current “skills/billing lanes” is vague drift bait. |
| P1-8 | Keep, tighten | Must be surgical edit to DAG definition/subset tests, not `prod_smoke_test.py` rewrite. |
| P1-9 | Split/retier | Split into agent:auto `P1-9a dev compose wiring disabled/opt-in config + tests` and tier:X `P1-9b operator starts dev stack and verifies live completion`. |
| P1-10 | Split/retier | Split backend metrics from docs honesty. Remove `TODO.md` from agent:auto or retier docs/TODO portion to X/Claude-owned. |
| G-1/G-2 | Keep tier:X | Correct. |
| H-* | Keep tier:X HOLD | Correct. |

**Exact AC Boundary Sentences To Add**
- P0-A: `MUST NOT refactor dag_storage, workflow step semantics, or existing callers beyond the minimal additive failed transition and nullable dag_task_id plumbing.`
- P0-3: `MUST NOT run or require a dev/staging/prod host migration; verification is limited to local alembic upgrade/downgrade tests and duplicate-revision checks.`
- P1-1: `MUST NOT execute DAG tasks, mark plans completed/failed, enable any systemd unit, call systemctl, or modify live host state; this ticket ships only an inert entrypoint and disabled unit template.`
- P1-2: `MUST NOT reuse or import the runner claim module/table; implement a dag_plans-local lease namespace only.`
- P1-3: `MUST NOT add parallel execution, retry policy, crash resume, or task handlers beyond scheduler ordering state needed by later tickets.`
- P1-4: `MUST NOT implement Gerrit push, registry publish, SSH, remote-board execution, hardware flash, package upload, or cross-compilation; unknown/nonlocal toolchains must fail the task.`
- P1-5: `MUST NOT add new export, billing, skills, retry, or side-effect behavior; this ticket only records per-task steps and terminal statuses.`
- P1-6: `MUST NOT contact real Gerrit, registry, SSH hosts, hardware, or prod databases during tests; use fake endpoints/env and assert the guard blocks before any network/action call.`
- P1-7: `MUST NOT change finetune selection semantics for non-executor completed runs.`
- P1-8: `MUST NOT rewrite prod_smoke_test.py orchestration, CLI parsing, polling, auth, or subset behavior; change only the DAG definition and focused tests needed for compile→run-test.`
- P1-9a: `MUST NOT start docker compose, enable systemd, require a docker socket, use staging/prod credentials, or verify against a live dev host; this ticket is repo wiring plus local config/tests only.`
- P1-10a: `MUST NOT edit TODO.md or product copy; this ticket only adds executor heartbeat/metric surfaces and tests.`
- P1-10b: `MUST NOT change executor code; this ticket only corrects operator/product documentation and is not runner-pickable if TODO.md is in scope.`

**Dependency Fixes**
Use real JIRA Blocks links:
- `P0-A → P1-5`
- `P0-3 → P1-1 → P1-2 → P1-3 → P1-4 → P1-5`
- `P1-4 → P1-6`
- `P1-4 → P1-8`
- `P1-5 → P1-7`
- `P1-5,P1-6,P1-8 → P1-9a`
- `P1-9a → P1-9b(tier:X) → G-1 → G-2`
- `P1-5 → P1-10a`
- docs/TODO honesty ticket depends on P1-5 or P1-9a, depending on wording.

The core plan is good. The draft needs these AC and tier changes before filing, otherwise runners will either overbuild the executor, claim host-only work, or mark disabled code as “deployed” without honest exercise evidence.
